PUBLISHERS NOTE 

The late Mr. SIMON ALEXANDER HEWAVITARANE, who 
in his life time had begun to publish the TRIPITAKA, left a bequest 
for its completion after his death. He died suddenly on 17th January 
1913 and in accordance with his wishes his Trustees had published the 
Commentaries on the Text in 49 volumes. After seventy years these 
books are no longer readily available. 

His widow Mrs. SOMAWAT1 HEWAVITARANE who passed away 
on 10th January 1982 at the age of 94, had prior to her death, created 
the SOMAWATI HEWAVITARANE TRUST to carry on the good work 
started by her late husband and bequeathed her entire estate to this Trust. 

We the Trustees of the Somawati Hewavitarane Trust, having seen the 
need to re-print the Commentaries, have undertaken this task to help both 
the student and teacher .of the Dhamma and in the hope that this act of 
ours will help to preserve the Noble Teachings of the Blessed One* for 
the benefit of the future generations and thereby prolong the life of the 
Buddha Sasana by many more thousands of years. 

May the fruits of this meritorious act help the late Simon Alexander 
Hewavitarane and Sjomawati Hewavitarane Maha Upasikawa in Sansara 
and towards their final attainment of Nibbana. 

Noel Wijenaika 
Nanda Amerasinghe 
Parinda Ranasinghe 


06 - 03-2009 

Somawati Hewavitarane Trust Office, 
130, Maligakanda Road, Colombo- 10, 
SRI LANKA. 



| ToC | 

PREFATORY NOTE. 


With the publication of the sixth part of the JStaka 
Atthakatba, beginning with the 40th Canto [Nipata] and ending 
with 80th, I have much pleasure in placing before the public the 
39th volume of the Simon Hewavitarne Bequest Series. The 
editing of this volume has also been done by the Ven’ble Pandit 
Widurupola Piyatissa Maha Nayaka Thero of the Up-country 
Amarapura Sect, Bambarakele Vihare, N’Eliya. I beg to convey 
my heartfelt thanks to the Revd. Editor who edited this volume 
with great persona! inconvedience having always a bnsy life. 

I have much pleasure to announce that the seventh or the 
last part of the Jataka Commentary, the MahA Nipata and second 
part of Pancappakarana AtthakathS are well in hand and will be 
out during the course of next year. Further I wish to mention here 
with the profound sorrow that the 25th anniversary of the death of 
Late Mr. Simon Hewavitarne, the benefactor of this Bequest Series, 
falls on the 17th January 1938. 

S. K. Moonesinghe. 

Tripitaka Publication Oft ce , 

Maligakanda, Colombo. 

20th December 

1937
